- **scrna-seq-qc**: Process, quality-control, annotate, and visualize single-cell or single-nucleus RNA-seq datasets across tissues and species. Use when Codex needs to build, adapt, or review a general scRNA-seq QC pipeline; choose dataset-appropriate cell-level filters from QC distributions; run required scDblFinder-based doublet and ambient-RNA filtering; annotate cells with matched references or marker-based fallbacks; or generate global and per-group UMAP visualizations for large scRNA-seq datasets. ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ngs-analysis/skills/scrna-seq-qc) · ★8k · 18 copies)
- **scrna-seq-qc-filtering-and-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq count matrices (e.g. filtered_feature_bc_matrix.h5 plus ATAC fragments .tsv.gz) must be QC-filtered and normalized in R/Seurat/Signac before multimodal integration or pairing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-qc-filtering-and-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control**: Use when reprocessing 10x Genomics 3'-end scRNA-seq FASTQs with scUTRquant and you must verify that gene counts from a truncated UTRome reference remain concordant with Cell Ranger before trusting downstream UTR-isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control-and-imputation**: Use when input is a raw gene-by-cell expression profile plus per-cell perturbation annotations (e.g. crop_stimulated or 10X perturb_GSM2396857 format) that must be QC'd and imputed before MUSIC topic modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control-and-imputation))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (10x-formatted mtx directory or CSV) must be filtered for cells with &lt;200 expressed genes, genes in &lt;3 cells, and high mitochondrial fractions before normalization and clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering--4efa74601c8e028c))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when starting from raw single-cell count matrices (e.g. .h5ad from GEO deposits like GSE149614 or Figshare-hosted atlas datasets) that need cleaning before batch correction, clustering, or differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering--3e6f71a0adb901d0))
- **scrna-seq-quality-matrix-construction**: Use when building the compact percentile-coverage matrix from SkewC gene body coverage output (coverage.r) as input for SkewC plotting, trim clustering, and typical-vs-skewed cell classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-matrix-construction))
- **scrna-seq-rare-cell-scoring**: Use when the goal is to detect rare cell populations in a scRNA-seq count matrix (thousands to ~68k+ profiles) and no annotated clusters exist yet; FiRE gives a per-cell continuous rareness score in seconds where RaceID/GiniClust scale poorly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-rare-cell-scoring))
- **scrna-seq-read-pseudoalignment**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads (e.g., 10x Genomics v3 FASTQ, such as sample GSM4626009 from GSE152766) need fast alignment and quantification into spliced and unspliced count matrices for downstream quality filtering with COPILOT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-read-pseudoalignment))
- **scrna-seq-run-statistics-collection**: Use when a scKB pseudoalignment run on 10xv3 FASTQ data (e.g. the 80 MB toy_data from GSM4626009, GSE152766) has finished and you must verify and gather its 8 output files for downstream COPILOT input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-run-statistics-collection))
- **scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing scalability of clustering or cluster-merging methods on scRNA-seq data: you need wall-clock runtime on a fixed, small, cell-type-labelled subset to contrast with AUROC performance on the full dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking--bce9883289b40420))
- **scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the runtime and memory footprint of pluripotency/scRNA-seq scoring tools (ORIGINS, LandSCENT SR, CytoTRACE) on GEO count matrices (breast GSE113197, colon GSE125970, hematopoietic GSE117498, lung GSE161383). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking--983ec1f0f6552bba))
- **scrna-seq-self-supervised-representation-learning**: Use when you need to cluster or embed a large scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. tens of thousands of cells, like the 43,095-cell 10x PBMC dataset) and standard pipelines underperform, or to benchmark deep-learning representation learning against Scanpy/Seurat/scVI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-self-supervised-representation-learning))
- **scrna-seq-similarity-learning**: Use when clustering scRNA-seq data (cells x genes matrix with ground-truth labels available for evaluation) and noisy/high-dropout expression makes naive cell-to-cell similarity unreliable; need NMI/ARI gains from similarity learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-similarity-learning))
- **scrna-seq-sparsity-comparison-across-technologies**: Use when comparing synthetic-data quality from small pilot sub-samples between scRNA-seq technologies (10x Genomics vs Smart-seq2), specifically to quantify whether sparsity degrades small-to-large inference via DBI, ARI, and cluster-proportion metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-sparsity-comparison-across-technologies))
- **scrna-seq-spatial-mapping**: Use when you need to link scRNA-seq cells to spatial coordinates/positions via optimal transport — e.g., mapping 1297 Drosophila embryo cells onto 3039 seqFISH/in situ spatial positions from geometry.txt and dge_normalized.txt/bdtnp.txt inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-spatial-mapping))
- **scrna-seq-spectral-clustering-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix with known cell-subpopulation labels (e.g., embryonic stages) is available and you must quantify whether imputation improves cell-population clustering accuracy against no-imputation and competitor imputers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-spectral-clustering-accuracy-evaluation))
- **scrna-seq-splicing-status-prediction**: Use when scRNA-seq reads labeled 'ambiguous' (compatible with both spliced and unspliced transcripts) need a probabilistic spliced/unspliced resolution, e.g. PBMC1k-style reads aligned to a spliceu augmented reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-splicing-status-prediction))
- **scrna-seq-stage-wise-dice-dynamics**: Use when a time-course or staged single-cell RNA-Seq dataset spans a known or suspected lineage bifurcation (e.g., retinal progenitors splitting into neuronal vs epithelial fates) and the exact transition/tipping stage must be resolved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-stage-wise-dice-dynamics))
- **scrna-seq-stemness-scoring**: Use when a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq expression matrix must be ranked by pluripotency/stemness to identify stem or progenitor cells, e.g. to pick trajectory-inference origins or to check whether a potency method recovers a known cellular hierarchy (HSC &gt; MPP &gt; GMP, stem vs TA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-stemness-scoring))
- **scrna-seq-stratified-cross-validation**: Use when benchmarking supervised cell-type identification methods on labeled scRNA-seq datasets and a fair, repeated train/test comparison of accuracy across datasets is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-stratified-cross-validation))
- **scrna-seq-study-data-preparation**: Use when importing new scRNA-seq (RNA-, ATAC-, or CITE-seq) studies into Cellenium, i.e. when raw/normalized single-cell data must be converted to annotated H5AD files with semantically organized study metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-study-data-preparation))
- **scrna-seq-subclone-clustering-tsne-visualization**: Use when deposited scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., drug-sensitive vs. resistant HMCL pairs like U266P vs U266VR) must be clustered into subclones and screened for drug-target gene enrichment to prioritize combination therapies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-subclone-clustering-tsne-visualization))
- **scrna-seq-subset-sampling**: Use when benchmarking the wall-clock runtime of scRNA-seq imputation methods (ALRA, MAGIC, DCA, scImpute, SAVER) as a function of cell count, requiring random subsets of a large filtered expression matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-subset-sampling))
- **scrna-seq-technology-parameter-extraction**: Use when building a reference table of supported single-cell RNA-seq chemistries and their barcode/UMI read-layout settings, or when verifying which technology a FASTQ input corresponds to. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-technology-parameter-extraction))
- **scrna-seq-temporal-data-handling**: Use when you have metabolic labeling scRNA-seq data (e.g., scEU-seq rpeLabeling.h5ad with separate labeled, unlabeled, and total count matrices plus a labeling-time vector) and need paired time-point inputs for RNAForecaster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-temporal-data-handling))
- **scrna-seq-to-bulk-3prime-seq-concordance**: Use when scUTRquant 3'UTR isoform quantification from scRNA-seq must be validated against bulk 3'-seq reference TPMs (e.g., ArrayExpress E-MTAB-7391 HSC or PolyASite-annotated ESC bulk data) to establish concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-to-bulk-3prime-seq-concordance))
- **scrna-seq-to-scatac-seq-label-transfer**: Use when integrating unpaired scRNA-seq (annotated source, h5ad with obs['CellType']) and scATAC-seq (unlabeled target) data to transfer cell-type labels, e.g. SNARE-seq or mouse-atlas benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-to-scatac-seq-label-transfer))
- **scrna-seq-train-test-split-preparation**: Use when a labeled cancer scRNA-seq dataset must be partitioned into training and testing sets to benchmark cell-type or cell-subtype annotation tools on the same cells, e.g., before computing per-cluster accuracy/F1 against original study annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-train-test-split-preparation))
- **scrna-seq-training-dataset-construction**: Use when building a supervised malignant-vs-normal epithelial training set from labeled scRNA-seq datasets where the raw 'Malignant'/'Tumor' annotations may be noisy and need refinement via cancer signature activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-training-dataset-construction))
- **scrna-seq-uncertainty-measurement**: Use when a pre-trained source classifier's soft probability outputs exist for scRNA-seq target cells and you need a per-cell uncertainty score to detect which cells belong to known cell types versus novel types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-uncertainty-measurement))
- **scrna-seq-variational-autoencoder-training**: Use when a UMI count matrix (cells x genes) needs low-dimensional embedding for visualization, batch correction across multiple batch factors (e.g., 30 patients x 3 health statuses x 2 locations), or mapping of held-out individuals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-variational-autoencoder-training))
- **scrna-seq-yan-dataset-processing**: Use when a Yan (or similar scRNA-seq) expression matrix and SSRE-inferred cluster labels are available and the goal is validating that the clustering supports downstream DEG detection via Kruskal-Wallis testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-yan-dataset-processing))
- **scrna-seq-zero-entry-partitioning**: Use when preprocessing a sparse scRNA-seq count matrix before imputation and you need to decide, per zero entry, whether it is a dropout event or a true zero rather than imputing all zeros indiscriminately. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-zero-entry-partitioning))
- **scrna-seurat-basics**: Use when the user references `生信教程` 第9-11课附近的单细胞基础、10x 读取、`CreateSeuratObject`、课堂 R/Seurat 入门和最基本对象创建流程。 ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/scrna-seurat-basics))
- **scrna-significant-violin-plots**: Seurat and ggplot2 violin-plot significance annotation for single-cell expression comparisons. Use when adding per-cell-type group comparisons to VlnPlot or geom_violin outputs, extracting gene expression from Seurat objects, running Wilcoxon tests with BH correction, placing significance stars above violins, exporting per-gene PDF or CSV outputs, or adapting classroom or pasted scRNA visualization snippets into reusable code. ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/scrna-significant-violin-plots))
- **scrna-stage-aggregation-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq AnnData object already contains per-cell TEI (or similar gene age-weighted index) in obs and the goal is to summarize and plot mean index per developmental stage to detect an hourglass pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-stage-aggregation-analysis))
- **scrna-trajectory-inference**: Reconstruct differentiation trajectories from scRNA-seq using PAGA, diffusion pseudotime, scVelo RNA velocity, and CellRank fate probabilities. Use when ordering cells along a developmental axis, identifying branch points, or computing cell-fate probabilities. ([UViiVe/fleet-skills-public](https://github.com/UViiVe/fleet-skills-public/tree/HEAD/scrna-trajectory-inference))
- **scrna-trajectory-monocle**: Use when the user references `生信教程` 第17-19课的 `monocle2`、`monocle3`、伪时序、轨迹重建或从 Seurat 对象转到 Monocle 分析的流程。 ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/scrna-trajectory-monocle))
- **scrnametaboliclandscape**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/scrnametaboliclandscape) · ★745)
- **scrnametaboliclandscape**: Comprehensive metabolic landscape analysis pipeline for scRNA-seq data. This is an all-in-one process group performing complete metabolic pathway analysis including expression imputation, feature selection, pathway activity calculation, and heterogeneity analysis. Based on methodology from Xiao et al. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-ml/scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2) · ★30 · 6 copies)
- **scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2) · ★745)
- **scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2f974fd5**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2f974fd5) · ★745)
- **scrnaseq-accession-data-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark requires the original Yan, Goolam, or Deng embryo scRNA-seq count matrices and only a GEO/ArrayExpress accession (e.g. GSE36552) is known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-accession-data-retrieval))
- **scrnaseq-alignment-with-star**: Use when input is paired Smart-Seq single-cell RNA-seq data named *_1.fastq/*_2.fastq and a genome reference (.fa) plus GTF are available, and per-cell alignments are needed for fusion or splice-aware analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-alignment-with-star))
- **scrnaseq-annadata-construction**: Use when raw scRNA-seq studies must be combined into a single AnnData with explicit batch (source study) and cell-type annotation columns before training scETM or running batch-integration benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-annadata-construction))
- **scrnaseq-annotation-audit**: Audit cell type annotations against h5ad expression data. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-annotation-audit))
- **scrnaseq-auto-annotation**: Use when running scRNAseq.py auto mode for LLM annotation. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-auto-annotation))
- **scrnaseq-batch-effect-quantification**: Use when comparing batch-integration/dimension-reduction methods (e.g., corralm, corralm+Harmony, corralm+MNN, multibatch PCA, Seurat) on multi-batch scRNA-seq count matrices and you need paired metrics of cell-type separation (ASW_celltype) vs batch mixing (1-ASWbatch). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-batch-effect-quantification))
- **scrnaseq-batch-integration-mixing-evaluation**: Use when two scRNA-seq datasets from different species (e.g., human and mouse primary motor cortex raw counts) must be integrated into one latent space and you need a mixing metric plus cross-species cell-type matching recall to judge embedding quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-batch-integration-mixing-evaluation))
- **scrnaseq-batch-integration-model-training**: Use when you have raw count matrices from multiple scRNA-seq batches (e.g. .h5ad AnnData, ≥2 batches, up to ~1M cells) and need an unsupervised integrated embedding plus an ablation-based attribution of which adversarial components drive batch correction vs bio-conservation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-batch-integration-model-training))
- **scrnaseq-bifurcation-trajectory-benchmarking**: Use when comparing accuracy of pseudotime inferred by bifurcation-capable trajectory methods on a single-cell RNA-seq differentiation dataset, using marker genes as an external pseudotime reference (e.g. Pearson/Spearman/Kendall correlation with ENO3, MEF2C, MYH2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-bifurcation-trajectory-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-cell-cycle-pseudo-time-recovery**: Use when you have unsynchronized scRNA-seq expression data and need to order cells along the cell cycle (G1/S/G2-M) or associate another single-cell modality (e.g., bisulfite methylation) with a recovered cell cycle pseudo time-series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-cycle-pseudo-time-recovery))
- **scrnaseq-cell-group-label-permutation-simulation**: Use when running NeuronChat (or its shared CellChat/CellPhoneDB workflow) on scRNA-seq or spatial transcriptomics and you must decide which inferred ligand-receptor communication links are statistically significant, or when testing p-value stability across permutation counts (M=100 vs M=1000). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-group-label-permutation-simulation))
- **scrnaseq-cell-subsampling-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when you must determine the minimum cell count needed for reliable logic-gate/GRN inference from scRNA-seq, or characterize how inference quality and runtime saturate with sample size and candidate TF-set size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-subsampling-sensitivity-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-cell-type-count-aggregation**: Use when you have cell-type-annotated scRNA-seq data across multiple samples/conditions and need to build a cell count matrix (e.g., via sccoda.util.cell_composition_data.from_pandas or from_scanpy) as input for compositional differential abundance analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-type-count-aggregation))
- **scrnaseq-cell-type-label-assessment**: Use when you need to quantitatively judge how well a dimension-reduction/visualization method separates known cell-type labels in scRNA-seq data, e.g. benchmarking an embedding against PCA, t-SNE, UMAP, or SCANPY. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-type-label-assessment))
- **scrnaseq-cluster-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing automatic cell type annotation of scRNA-seq clusters (scCATCH vs Seurat-based one-against-all markers), especially to quantify pseudo marker gene effects on annotation consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cluster-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-cluster-stability-assessment**: Use when scRNAseq clusters must be validated for reconstruction robustness — e.g. after clustering with an SCA-encoded space (TF/miRNA/kinase targets) on datasets like setA, or when comparing a variant latent space (e.g. vSCA) against a baseline SCA run via QCM/QCC trajectory superimposability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cluster-stability-assessment))
- **scrnaseq-clustering-ensemble-implementation**: Use when multiple scRNAseq clustering results must be integrated into one consensus cell-assignment set via average similarity on binary matrices, with silhouette-guided k selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrnaseq-clustering-ensemble-implementation))
- **scrnaseq-clustering-quality-diagnosis**: Use when scRNA-seq Leiden clustering looks wrong. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/scrnaseq-clustering-quality-diagnosis))
- **scrnaseq-compositional-analysis**: scRNA-seq DA via scCODA for cell-type proportions. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-compositional-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-condition-wise-subsetting**: Use when a pooled single-condition-agnostic scRNA-seq matrix with cell-type annotations and patient/condition labels must be divided into separate disease-state groups before per-group ligand-receptor inference (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-condition-wise-subsetting))
- **scrnaseq-confounder-corrected-pca**: Use when preprocessing 10X scRNA-seq matrices (matrix.mtx/gene.tsv/barcode.tsv) prior to clustering, to correct confounders like cell cycle or mitochondrial load and to choose the number of PCs via shuffled-data simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-confounder-corrected-pca))
- **scrnaseq-confounding-factor-correction**: Use when a scRNAseq expression matrix shows unwanted variation (e.g. ERCC spike-in confounding or cell-cycle effects) and downstream clustering must recover known biological groupings from corrected data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-confounding-factor-correction))
- **scrnaseq-count-aggregation-by-cell-type**: Use when a scRNA-seq anndata (.h5ad) matrix with per-cell metadata (cell_type, donor_id, condition) must be reduced to pseudo-bulk counts per sample and cell type before calling DEGs between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-aggregation-by-cell-type))
- **scrnaseq-count-data-modeling**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices need a denoised, low-dimensional latent feature representation that explicitly models zero inflation (dropout) before downstream cell-type clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-data-modeling))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-loading**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices must be ingested into R before SCEVAN analysis — e.g., GSE131928 GBM samples (MGH102, MGH104, MGH105, MGH106) or multiple myeloma 10X data destined for pipelineCNA or multiSampleComparisonClonalCN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-loading))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-melting**: Use when a wide-format scRNA-seq count matrix (.csv) with one column per gene must be reshaped into a tall cell-gene-value table before Parquet serialization and downstream scSPARKL analysis on low-memory hardware. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-melting))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization**: Use when a raw or unnormalized scRNA-seq count matrix from two biological conditions (e.g. treated vs untreated cells) must be normalized in Seurat before calling differentially expressed genes for downstream regulatory-element queries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization**: Use when you have a per-cell sparse k-mer abundance matrix (from jellyfish counts) and a pre-built CKG look-up table, and need reference-free CKG-level cell features for downstream cell type classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when input is a raw scRNA-Seq count matrix (gene x cell CSV) that must be quality-filtered and reduced to highly variable genes before imputation or K-means clustering evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--fb45dcfeca37a037))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw unfiltered droplet-based scRNA-seq count matrix (.h5 or .mtx) must be shaped for EmptyNN cell-calling or PU-learning based filtering of cell-free droplets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--10b88a3414841bc2))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (e.g. GEO accession like GSE81682 mouse HSPC) must be normalized and converted with cellexalvrR into CellexalVR-consumable input files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--b5ce2bfe69570a2e))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix is sparse and dropout-prone and must be filtered, HVG-selected, and normalized to [0,1] before binarization or landmark construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--6ae154ee4e887cb1))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-processing**: Use when two cross-species scRNA-seq datasets (count matrices) must be preprocessed and paired with a gene similarity distance matrix as input to a cell type matching model such as TACTiCS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-processing))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when you need a simulated scRNA-seq benchmark dataset with known cell-type labels and controllable cluster sizes (e.g., a rare population &lt;5% of cells) to evaluate clustering accuracy and robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-simulation--6f66c5e05e736952))
